# Lock comparison: `with-our-stack` against `main`

Generated by `npm run compare` from the pipeline's outputs on both locks; don't edit it. The data is in [`with-our-stack.json`](with-our-stack.json).

| Lock | Commit | Spec blob | IR SHA-256 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `main` | `44dfaaf` | `69409583` | `c3e278884979` |
| `with-our-stack` | `711b34c` | `c395ca84` | `943d8f8d5338` |

## Summary

- **Specification:** 1875 elements added, 123 removed, and 146 changed.
- **Schema changes,** as the release differ classes them from `main` to `with-our-stack`: in the model, 277 breaking, 341 additive, and 79 annotation; in the rules, 13, 66, and 0; in the schema files, 95, 178, and 0.
- **Statements:** 747 placed that `main` lacks, 31 gone, and 2 placed elsewhere.
- **Decisions:** 17 cited only with `with-our-stack`, 36 only on `main`, and 4 cited on both whose covered text differs.
- **Findings:** spec lint 292 added, 46 removed, 1 changed; examples 0 added, 58 removed; round trip 0 added, 4 removed, 0 changed; conditions left to the rules 51 added, 0 removed.

## Specification elements

Elements by IR ID. An element is changed when its digest differs: a table row's source bytes, or any other element's content.

### Added (1875)

#### field (48)

| ID | Text |
| :--- | :--- |
| `field:4.1:assent[]` | `assent[]` \| Array[AssentEntry] \| recorded \| The parties' acts (§ 4.1.2). Each entry is written whole by the party its `role` implies. Entries MUST NOT be edited or removed |
| `field:4.1:brief_id` | `brief_id` \| String \| immutable \| The buyer's brief this proposal answers |
| `field:4.1:buyer` | `buyer` \| Party \| immutable \| The entity that assents, usually the agency. Its presence makes the proposal issued to that buyer (§ 4.3). Whom the spend is for is `commitment.advertisers[]` (§ 5.6.1), and whether the buyer may bind that advertiser is authorization (§ 1.2). `legal_name` is REQUIRED here |
| `field:4.1:ext` | `ext` \| Ext \| seller-set \| See Extensions (§ 2.5) |
| `field:4.1:issued_from` | `issued_from` \| VersionRef \| immutable \| The shared proposal and version an issued proposal was made from (§ 4.3). A pointer: nothing is inherited through it |
| `field:4.1:line_items[]` | `line_items[]` \| Array[LineItem] \| seller-set \| The line items (§ 5), in the order the seller wrote them. A proposal holds `line_items[]` or `packages[]`, not both |
| `field:4.1:offer_window` | `offer_window` \| Interval \| seller-set \| The window the offer holds: it does not hold before `start` or after `end`. This is the offer window, not the campaign flight |
| `field:4.1:packages[]` | `packages[]` \| Array[Package] \| seller-set \| The line items in packages (§ 4.1.4), in the order the seller wrote them |
| `field:4.1:proposal_template_id` | `proposal_template_id` \| String \| immutable \| Links a proposal to the seller's template. Opaque to buyers; used for the seller's reporting |
| `field:4.1:seller` | `seller` \| Party \| immutable \| The selling entity. `seller.domain` is the seller's identifier for all purposes, including `assent[]`. `legal_name` is REQUIRED here |
| `field:4.1:spec_version` | `spec_version` \| String \| seller-set \| The version of this specification the document follows, such as `3.0` |
| `field:4.2:media_kit_detail` | `media_kit_detail` \| String \| seller-set \| Detail at media-kit depth |
| `field:4.4:change_requests[]` | `change_requests[]` \| Array[ChangeRequest] \| recorded \| Ordered post-`agreed` history. Each entry carries the fields of the OpenDirect 2.1 ChangeRequest, except its `webhook`, plus `baseline`, `actor`, `at`, and `cancellation`. Outside the binding record (§ 4.1.1) and the offered terms (§ 4.1.3), like `negotiation_history[]`, so an entry changes neither `record_digest` nor `version` |
| `field:5.10:agreement` | `agreement` \| Agreement \| seller-set \| The master or annual agreement the line item is priced under. An informative pointer; the agreement itself is outside this specification. A buyer-held agreement id travels as an `external_references[]` entry with `reference_type: agreement` |
| `field:5.1:ext` | `ext` \| Ext \| seller-set \| See Extensions (§ 2.5) |
| `field:5.2:break_position` | `break_position` \| Selectable[BreakPositionOption] \| selectable \| Where in the content stream an ad plays, on `video`, `ctv`, and `audio` line items. See 5.2.3. Absent, or nothing in effect: the seller promises no position |
| `field:5.2:episodes` | `episodes` \| Enum \| seller-set \| `new` \| `catalog` \| `all`. `new`: released within the flight. `catalog`: released before it. `all`: either |
| `field:5.2:placement` | `placement` \| Placement \| seller-set \| What every impression of this line item is: rewarded, interstitial, video placement subtype, skippability, playback, API frameworks, refresh, server-side insertion and audio feed. See 5.2.2 |
| `field:5.2:programs` | `programs` \| Selectable[ProgramOption] \| selectable \| The content the line item serves within. See 5.2.9. Absent, or nothing in effect: no content is promised |
| `field:5.4:creative_policy` | `creative_policy` \| String \| seller-set \| Content and category restrictions on creative, in prose |
| `field:5.4:format_specs` | `format_specs` \| Map[FormatId, FormatSpec] \| seller-set \| Map from format id to `FormatSpec` |
| `field:5.5:accepted_exclusions` | `accepted_exclusions` \| AcceptedExclusions \| seller-set \| What the seller can enforce. See below |
| `field:5.5:exclusions` | `exclusions` \| Map[ExclusionType, ExclusionList] \| supplied \| The buyer's block lists, keyed by exclusion type. See below |
| `field:5.5:markets` | `markets` \| Selectable[GeoCode] \| selectable \| For a line item priced by market. See `GeoCode` below |
| `field:5.5:time_zone` | `time_zone` \| ZoneName \| seller-set \| The clock for `daypart` and a calendar `frequency_cap.period` |
| `field:5.6:delivery_counting` | `delivery_counting` \| DeliveryCounting \| seller-set \| How the line item counts plays and, when it counts impressions, turns them into impressions. See 5.6.4 |
| `field:5.6:hold` | `hold` \| Hold \| lifecycle \| The buyer's hold. Absent until the buyer first asks. See 5.6.2 |
| `field:5.6:hold_terms` | `hold_terms` \| HoldTerms \| seller-set \| Offers a hold. Absent means `available` is `false`. See 5.6.2 |
| `field:5.6:preemption` | `preemption` \| Preemption \| seller-set \| See § 5.6.7. Absent: no later sale preempts this line item |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].cost_definition` | `pricing[].cost_definition` \| DocumentRef \| seller-set \| Public definition of one unit and the `cost_parameters` keys (§ 5.6.5) |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].cost_parameters` | `pricing[].cost_parameters` \| CostParameters \| seller-set \| The keys the term requires (§ 5.6.5). Absent when it requires none |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].list_rate` | `pricing[].list_rate` \| Amount \| requestable \| The rate before options. A buyer MAY request another value, and a granted value is the `list_rate` of the next `version` |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].min_rate` | `pricing[].min_rate` \| Amount \| seller-set \| A hint: the lowest `list_rate` the seller says it will consider. It grants nothing. Not a term, not the downstream auction floor, and never sent downstream |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].price_type` | `pricing[].price_type` \| Integer \| seller-set \| How `agreed_rate` clears, as Deals API 1.0 `terms.pricetype`: Dynamic (0), the auction type of each bid request; First Price (1); Second Price Plus (2); Fixed Price (3); or an exchange-specific value, 500 and up |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].pricing_id` | `pricing[].pricing_id` \| String \| immutable \| Names the entry within the line item. A revision MUST NOT reuse it for a different `cost_method`, `cost_parameters`, `currency`, or `transaction_mechanisms[]` |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].rate_per` | `pricing[].rate_per` \| Integer \| seller-set \| `1` \| `1000`. Units per rate. For a core term, the vocabulary sets it, and a present value MUST match |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].transaction_mechanisms[]` | `pricing[].transaction_mechanisms[]` \| Array[TransactionMechanism] \| seller-set \| The `transaction_mechanism` options this entry prices. Absent: every offered option |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].unit_bounds` | `pricing[].unit_bounds` \| Range[Integer] \| requestable \| Bounds on the units committed under this entry, counted in its unit (§ 5.6.5): `commitment.amount` when `basis` is `units`, otherwise `derived_units`. A buyer MAY request another. Absent on a `FLAT` entry |
| `field:5.6:pricing[].unit_label` | `pricing[].unit_label` \| String \| seller-set \| A short label for one unit, such as `engagement` |
| `field:5.6:renewal_right` | `renewal_right` \| VocabularyTerm \| seller-set \| The current advertiser's right to the next cycle. Core term: `first_refusal`, the right to buy the next cycle before it opens up. Absent: no such right. It is not a place in a hold queue (`hold.position`) |
| `field:5.7:controlling_measurement` | `controlling_measurement` \| Selectable[ControllingMeasurementOption] \| selectable \| Whose count of billable units is invoiced. See § 5.7.1. `max_select: 1`. `included[]` MUST name exactly one option |
| `field:5.8:eligible_buyers[]` | `eligible_buyers[]` \| Array[RegistrableDomain] \| seller-set \| The buyers that may commit, by `buyer.domain` |
| `field:5.8:eligible_seats[]` | `eligible_seats[]` \| Array[SeatRef] \| seller-set \| The seats that may commit. An entry with an absent or empty `ids[]` names no seat and admits no one, unlike an absent OpenRTB 2.6 `Deal.wseat`, which sets no restriction |
| `field:5.8:terms_refs[]` | `terms_refs[]` \| Array[DocumentRef] \| seller-set \| The binding documents of this line item, such as a main agreement and its addenda, or an IO template. Every entry applies, with no AND or OR. A `commitment` entry accepts them all (§ 4.1.2). Absent: a `commitment` entry accepts no document |
| `field:5.9:deal_origin` | `deal_origin` \| RegistrableDomain \| seller-set \| Absent otherwise. The advertising system domain whose namespace holds the deal ID and that pushes the deal, as the Deals API 1.0 `origin`: typically an SSP, or the ad server for a deal it books itself |
| `field:5.9:retention` | `retention` \| Range[Duration] \| requestable \| From release or air. The ad stays in every copy of the content the seller distributes for at least `min` and is removed by `max`. No `max`: indefinitely. When `placement.feed` is present, only copies in that feed count toward delivery |
| `field:5.9:sov_basis` | `sov_basis` \| Enum \| seller-set \| `impressions` \| `loop_time`. `impressions`: a share of the line item's eligible impressions (digital placements, pooled DOOH products). `loop_time`: a share of each named screen's loop time (fixed-loop DOOH spots) |
| `field:5.9:sov_share` | `sov_share` \| Fraction \| seller-set \| Absent under `loop_time`. The promised share, greater than 0 |

| `key:4.4:ChangeRequest.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Opaque identifier of the change request |
| `key:4.4:ChangeRequest.lineid` | `lineid` \| OPTIONAL \| The Line in that Order, for a change at line level |

| `key:4.4:ChangeRequest.status` | `status` \| OPTIONAL \| The outcome of the request |
| `key:5.10:Agreement.agreement_id` | `agreement_id` \| REQUIRED \| The agreement's id |
| `key:5.10:Agreement.agreement_type` | `agreement_type` \| OPTIONAL \| Core terms: `upfront` \| `annual` \| `master_services`. A receiver that doesn't know the term still reads the `agreement` |
| `key:5.10:Agreement.document` | `document` \| OPTIONAL \| The agreement, where the parties pin it |
| `key:5.10:Agreement.parties[]` | `parties[]` \| OPTIONAL \| The agreement's parties |
| `key:5.10:Agreement.term` | `term` \| OPTIONAL \| When the agreement runs |
| `key:5.10:Billing.cycle_length` | `cycle_length` \| REQUIRED when `invoice_period` is `cycle` \| How long each `cycle` period runs, such as `P28D` (4 weeks) |
| `key:5.10:Billing.delivering_party` | `delivering_party` \| OPTIONAL \| Who delivers. Absent: the `seller` |
| `key:5.10:Billing.invoice_period` | `invoice_period` \| OPTIONAL \| The invoice period (§ 5.7.1). Core terms: `month` (default) \| `cycle` |
| `key:5.10:Billing.invoicing_party` | `invoicing_party` \| OPTIONAL \| Who invoices. Absent: the `seller` |
| `key:5.10:Billing.payment_terms` | `payment_terms` \| OPTIONAL \| The payment terms, such as `net 30` |
| `key:5.10:Billing.time_zone` | `time_zone` \| OPTIONAL \| The clock for a `month` invoice period. Absent: `UTC` |
| `key:5.10:ExternalReference.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| The id |
| `key:5.10:ExternalReference.namespace` | `namespace` \| OPTIONAL \| The namespace of the id in that system |
| `key:5.10:ExternalReference.reference_type` | `reference_type` \| REQUIRED \| What the id names. Core terms: `order` \| `line` \| `agreement` \| `deal`. `order`: the order that books the line item, such as an OpenDirect Order or an IO. `line`: the line in that order, such as an OpenDirect Line. `agreement`: a buyer-held agreement id. `deal`: the deal ID that bid requests carry, with `system` set to `deal_origin`. It is the Deals API `id` and the OpenRTB 2.6 `Deal.id`, whether `deal_origin` assigned it or accepted it from the seller. An ID that bid requests don't carry, such as the origin's internal record ID, is not a `deal` entry. A receiver that doesn't know the term ignores the entry |
| `key:5.10:ExternalReference.system` | `system` \| REQUIRED \| The system that holds the id |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.api[]` | `api[]` \| OPTIONAL \| Values from List: API Frameworks that every impression supports, such as MRAID 3.0 (6), OMID 1.0 (7) and SIMID 1.1 (9) \| `DisplayPlacement.api`, `VideoPlacement.api`, `AudioPlacement.api`, `banner.api`, `video.api`, `audio.api` |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.feed` | `feed` \| REQUIRED when `channel` is `audio` \| A value from List: Feed Types, such as Podcast (3) \| `AudioPlacement.feed`, `audio.feed` |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.instl` | `instl` \| OPTIONAL \| The ad is interstitial or full screen \| `DisplayPlacement.instl`, `imp.instl` |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.playmethod[]` | `playmethod[]` \| OPTIONAL \| Video or audio. Values from List: Playback Methods in use, such as Initiates without any specific user interaction with Sound On (1), Initiates without any specific user interaction Sound Off by Default (2), Initiates on Click with Sound On (3) and Continuous Playback (7) \| `VideoPlacement.playmethod`, `AudioPlacement.playmethod`, `video.playbackmethod` |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.plcmt` | `plcmt` \| REQUIRED when `channel` is `video` \| A value from List: Plcmt Subtypes - Video, such as Instream (1). Its presence states that the line item is video, so a `ctv` line item that sells video carries it \| `VideoPlacement.ptype`, `video.plcmt` |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.refresh` | `refresh` \| OPTIONAL \| How often the slot refreshes \| OpenRTB 2.6 only: `imp.refresh.refsettings[].minint` |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.reward` | `reward` \| OPTIONAL \| The user receives a reward for viewing the ad \| `Placement.reward`, `imp.rwdd` |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.skip` | `skip` \| OPTIONAL \| Video or audio. The placement lets the user skip the ad \| `VideoPlacement.skip`, `AudioPlacement.skip`, `video.skip` |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.skipafter` | `skipafter` \| OPTIONAL \| When `skip` is `1`: seconds the ad plays before the placement enables skipping \| `VideoPlacement.skipafter`, `AudioPlacement.skipafter`, `video.skipafter` |
| `key:5.2.2:Placement.ssai` | `ssai` \| OPTIONAL \| Whether server-side ad insertion is in use and what it does to asset and tracker retrieval, in the values of AdCOM 1.0 `Placement.ssai`, such as all server-side (3) \| `Placement.ssai`, `imp.ssai` |
| `key:5.2.2:Refresh.min_interval_s` | `min_interval_s` \| REQUIRED \| Minimum seconds a creative renders before the slot refreshes |
| `key:5.2.3:BreakPositionOption.delay` | `delay` \| OPTIONAL \| A value from List: Start Delay Modes, such as Pre-Roll (0) \| `delay`, `video.startdelay` / `audio.startdelay` |
| `key:5.2.3:BreakPositionOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1) \| none |
| `key:5.2.3:BreakPositionOption.maxseq` | `maxseq` \| OPTIONAL \| Ceiling: the most ads a served pod holds \| `maxseq`, `maxseq` |
| `key:5.2.3:BreakPositionOption.poddur` | `poddur` \| OPTIONAL \| Ceiling: the most seconds of dynamic ad time a served pod holds \| `poddur`, `poddur` |
| `key:5.2.3:BreakPositionOption.podseq` | `podseq` \| OPTIONAL \| A value from List: Pod Sequence \| `podseq`, `podseq` |
| `key:5.2.3:BreakPositionOption.slotinpod` | `slotinpod` \| OPTIONAL \| A value from List: Slot Position in Pod \| `slotinpod`, `slotinpod` |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.allocation` | `allocation` \| OPTIONAL \| `all` \| `seller_allocated`. Absent means `all`. With `all`, the line item runs on every listed screen. With `seller_allocated`, the seller picks the screens: `screens[]`, when present, is the pool it picks from and is seller-set, and the proof-of-play log names the screens used |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.apps[]` | `apps[]` \| REQUIRED when `domains[]` is absent \| The apps behind a property sold in `app_mobile` or `ctv` (§ 5.2.5) |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.child_audience` | `child_audience` \| REQUIRED \| `directed` \| `mixed` \| `general`. `directed`: children are its primary audience under the law the seller applies, or its app store lists it for children only. `mixed`: directed to children but not as its primary audience, or listed for children and older users. `general`: neither. § 5.2.7 states what follows |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.child_treatment` | `child_treatment` \| REQUIRED when `child_audience` is `mixed` \| `excluded` \| `contextual`. `excluded`: users identified as children don't receive the line item. `contextual`: they receive it without personal data |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.domains[]` | `domains[]` \| REQUIRED when `apps[]` is absent \| The property's domains. On a `dooh` line item, the inventory owner's domain, as OpenRTB 2.6 `dooh.domain` |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1) |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.inventory_partner_domain` | `inventory_partner_domain` \| OPTIONAL \| As OpenRTB 2.6 `site.inventorypartnerdomain` and `app.inventorypartnerdomain` |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.min_screens` | `min_screens` \| OPTIONAL \| With `seller_allocated`, the fewest screens the seller allocates to the line item |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.name` | `name` \| OPTIONAL \| The property's name, for people |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.screens[]` | `screens[]` \| REQUIRED when `channel` is `dooh` and `allocation` is `all` \| The property's screens (§ 5.2.8) |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.seller_accounts[]` | `seller_accounts[]` \| REQUIRED when `seller_relationship` is `reseller`, or the line item offers a deal mechanism (§ 5.9) \| The accounts that sell the property |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.seller_relationship` | `seller_relationship` \| REQUIRED \| `owner` \| `manager` \| `inventory_partner` \| `reseller`, checked as § 5.2.6 states |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.slots_per_loop` | `slots_per_loop` \| OPTIONAL \| The slots per loop the line item buys on each screen of the property that has a `loop` |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.sync_groups[]` | `sync_groups[]` \| OPTIONAL \| Screens that show the same ad at the same moment, sold as one (§ 5.2.8) |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.tv_channel` | `tv_channel` \| OPTIONAL \| The TV channel or station the property carries, as an AdCOM 1.0 `Channel` object in AdCOM's keys and meanings. It SHOULD carry `domain`. Properties whose `tv_channel.domain` is the same carry the same channel, so a buyer can tell when two sellers offer it. Each seller assigns its own `id`, so `id` matches only within one seller |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.tv_network` | `tv_network` \| OPTIONAL \| The network that owns the property's channel, as an AdCOM 1.0 `Network` object in AdCOM's keys and meanings, matched by `domain` the same way |
| `key:5.2.4:PropertyOption.venue_type` | `venue_type` \| REQUIRED when `channel` is `dooh` \| `taxonomy` is a value of AdCOM 1.0 List: DOOH Venue Taxonomies. `ids[]` holds exactly one id. It covers only itself, not its descendants. The values OpenRTB 2.6 `dooh.venuetypetax` and `dooh.venuetype` carry |
| `key:5.2.5:App.developer_domain` | `developer_domain` \| RECOMMENDED \| Developer domain, informative; the governing developer domain comes from the store listing. No OpenRTB equivalent: not `app.domain`, which is the domain of the app |
| `key:5.2.5:App.store_url` | `store_url` \| OPTIONAL \| Store listing URL. As OpenRTB 2.6 `app.storeurl` |
| `key:5.2.5:AppId.bundle` | `bundle` \| REQUIRED \| Store ID. As OpenRTB 2.6 `app.bundle` |
| `key:5.2.5:AppId.store` | `store` \| REQUIRED \| The app store that issued `bundle`. Core terms: `apple_app_store` \| `google_play` \| `roku_channel_store` \| `amazon_appstore` \| `samsung_apps_tv`. A `bundle` is unique only within its store. No OpenRTB equivalent |
| `key:5.2.6:SellerAccount.asi` | `asi` \| REQUIRED \| As ads.txt field 1, Domain name of the advertising system |
| `key:5.2.6:SellerAccount.sid` | `sid` \| REQUIRED \| As ads.txt field 2, Publisher's Account ID |
| `key:5.2.8:Loop.length_s` | `length_s` \| REQUIRED \| Loop length, in seconds, counting every item the loop plays, paid or not |
| `key:5.2.8:Loop.slot_s` | `slot_s` \| REQUIRED \| Slot length, in seconds |
| `key:5.2.8:Screen.lat` | `lat` \| OPTIONAL \| Latitude, as OpenRTB 2.6 `geo.lat`. Absent when `movable` is `true` |
| `key:5.2.8:Screen.lon` | `lon` \| OPTIONAL \| Longitude, as OpenRTB 2.6 `geo.lon`. Absent when `movable` is `true` |
| `key:5.2.8:Screen.loop` | `loop` \| OPTIONAL \| Absent when the screen schedules ads without a loop |
| `key:5.2.8:Screen.movable` | `movable` \| OPTIONAL \| True when the screen has no fixed location, as AdCOM 1.0 `Dooh.fixed` value 2 (movable) |
| `key:5.2.8:Screen.operating_hours[]` | `operating_hours[]` \| OPTIONAL \| `ClockWindow` entries (§ 5.5), in the screen's `time_zone` |
| `key:5.2.8:Screen.screen_id` | `screen_id` \| REQUIRED \| The seller's id for the screen, as its proof-of-play log carries it. When the bid request's `device.ifa_type` is `ppid`, as the OpenRTB 2.6 implementation guide (§ 7.9.2.5) uses it, `device.ifa` carries the same id |
| `key:5.2.8:Screen.time_zone` | `time_zone` \| OPTIONAL \| The screen's time zone |
| `key:5.2.8:SyncGroup.min_screens_per_play` | `min_screens_per_play` \| REQUIRED \| The fewest of the group's screens that must show a display for it to count as one play (§ 5.6.4) |
| `key:5.2.8:SyncGroup.screen_ids[]` | `screen_ids[]` \| REQUIRED \| The `screen_id` of each screen in the group |
| `key:5.2.8:SyncGroup.sync_group_id` | `sync_group_id` \| REQUIRED \| The group's id |
| `key:5.2.9:EventSchedule.eventstatus` | `eventstatus` \| RECOMMENDED \| `1` (scheduled) \| `2` (tentative) \| `3` (cancelled). Absent: `1` |
| `key:5.2.9:EventSchedule.flexibleend` | `flexibleend` \| OPTIONAL \| Whether `scheduledend` can move, which LEAP calls flexible. Absent: `0` |
| `key:5.2.9:EventSchedule.scheduledend` | `scheduledend` \| REQUIRED \| When the event is scheduled to end |
| `key:5.2.9:EventSchedule.scheduledstart` | `scheduledstart` \| REQUIRED \| When the event is scheduled to start |
| `key:5.2.9:ProgramOption.content` | `content` \| OPTIONAL \| An AdCOM 1.0 `Content` object, in AdCOM's keys and meanings, `series`, `title`, `network`, `channel`, `genres`, `rating`, `live`, and `realtime` included. Its `channel` is the AdCOM `Channel` object, the TV channel or station the content is on |
| `key:5.2.9:ProgramOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1). It is not compared with delivered content |
| `key:5.2.9:ProgramOption.schedule` | `schedule` \| OPTIONAL \| When the event airs. An option for a scheduled event carries it |
| `key:5.3:AddressableScale.quantity` | `quantity` \| REQUIRED \| How many |
| `key:5.3:AddressableScale.unit` | `unit` \| REQUIRED \| What `quantity` counts. Core terms: the `frequency_cap.per` terms (§ 5.5), and `person`. A receiver that doesn't know a term reads the figure as unstated |
| `key:5.3:AddressableScale.window` | `window` \| REQUIRED \| The dates `quantity` covers |
| `key:5.3:AudienceOption.data_label` | `data_label` \| REQUIRED \| The option's IAB Tech Lab Data Transparency Standard 1.1 label: every field in its "Minimum Segment Disclosures: Required Fields" table, keyed by its API flat file key, with values exactly as the standard publishes them, such as `Observed/Known` or `TCF (Europe)`. A multi-select field the standard gives no separator for is an array of those values. The standard's conditional rules apply: offline data sources require the Onboarder Details fields, which otherwise take the standard's `N/A` option; `Modeled` requires `audience_expansion: Yes`; and `Single domain / App` scope requires a domain or app store URL in `originating_domain` |
| `key:5.3:AudienceOption.excludable` | `excludable` \| OPTIONAL \| Whether the buyer may name this option in `excluded[]`. Absent means `false` |
| `key:5.3:AudienceOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1) |
| `key:5.3:AudienceOption.name` | `name` \| OPTIONAL \| The audience's name, for people |
| `key:5.3:AudienceOption.sensitive_categories[]` | `sensitive_categories[]` \| REQUIRED \| The categories the option's membership is derived from or reveals. Core terms: `racial_or_ethnic_origin` \| `political_opinions` \| `religious_or_philosophical_beliefs` \| `trade_union_membership` \| `genetic` \| `biometric` \| `health` \| `sex_life_or_sexual_orientation` \| `precise_geolocation` \| `minors` (under 18). An empty array declares none |
| `key:5.3:AudienceOption.taxonomy` | `taxonomy` \| REQUIRED \| The integer from the OpenRTB segment taxonomies list for the IDs that name this option: `4` (IAB Tech Lab Audience Taxonomy 1.1) for the label's `taxonomy_id_list`, or the seller's registered vendor value (500 and up) for the label's `audience_id`. When the seller signals the segment in an OpenRTB 2.6 request, these are the `data.ext.segtax` and `segment.id` values |
| `key:5.3:AudienceSelectable.combine` | `combine` \| REQUIRED when more than one seller audience can be in effect \| The operators the seller supports. `max_select: 1`. `included[]` MUST name exactly one option. It carries no `deltas` |
| `key:5.3:AudienceSelectable.excluded[]` | `excluded[]` \| OPTIONAL \| The buyer's write: option ids of options whose `excludable` is `true`, disjoint from the seller audiences in effect. `max_select` does not count them |
| `key:5.3:BuyerList.list_id` | `list_id` \| REQUIRED \| Identity of the list within the line item. The buyer uses it wherever the list's members travel |
| `key:5.3:BuyerList.match_method` | `match_method` \| OPTIONAL \| How its members match users. Core terms: `hashed_email` \| `device_id`. A receiver that doesn't know a term reads the method as unstated |
| `key:5.3:BuyerList.size` | `size` \| OPTIONAL \| How many members it holds |
| `key:5.3:BuyerList.source` | `source` \| OPTIONAL \| Where the list comes from, such as `advertiser_crm` |
| `key:5.3:BuyerSupplied.expected_match_rate` | `expected_match_rate` \| OPTIONAL \| The seller's stated expectation of the share of a buyer's list that matches |
| `key:5.3:BuyerSupplied.segments[]` | `segments[]` \| OPTIONAL \| The buyer's segments |
| `key:5.3:BuyerSupplied.segments_allowed` | `segments_allowed` \| OPTIONAL \| Whether the buyer may supply `segments[]` |
| `key:5.3:BuyerSupplied.suppression_allowed` | `suppression_allowed` \| OPTIONAL \| Whether the buyer may supply `suppression_list` |
| `key:5.3:BuyerSupplied.suppression_list` | `suppression_list` \| OPTIONAL \| The users the line item doesn't target |
| `key:5.4:Asset.approval_status` | `approval_status` \| OPTIONAL \| The seller's review outcome. The seller moves it |
| `key:5.4:Asset.asset_id` | `asset_id` \| REQUIRED \| Identity of the asset within the line item |
| `key:5.4:Asset.built_by` | `built_by` \| OPTIONAL \| Which side built it. Absent: the buyer |
| `key:5.4:Asset.format_id` | `format_id` \| REQUIRED \| The `formats.available[]` id the asset is for |
| `key:5.4:Asset.markup` | `markup` \| REQUIRED when `url` is absent \| The tag or creative markup |
| `key:5.4:Asset.provenance` | `provenance` \| OPTIONAL \| How the asset was made |
| `key:5.4:Asset.submitted_at` | `submitted_at` \| OPTIONAL \| When it was delivered |
| `key:5.4:Asset.url` | `url` \| REQUIRED when `markup` is absent \| Where the asset is, such as a tag URL with its macros unexpanded |
| `key:5.4:AssetProvenance.content_digest` | `content_digest` \| OPTIONAL \| Over the exact bytes served at `url` |
| `key:5.4:AssetProvenance.disclosure_methods[]` | `disclosure_methods[]` \| OPTIONAL \| How the content is marked or disclosed. Core terms: `c2pa_manifest` \| `watermark` \| `audible_notice` \| `visible_label`. A receiver that doesn't know a term reads that method as unstated |
| `key:5.4:AssetProvenance.generator` | `generator` \| OPTIONAL \| The system that generated it |
| `key:5.4:AssetProvenance.model_version` | `model_version` \| OPTIONAL \| The version of the model that generated it |
| `key:5.4:AssetProvenance.synthetic` | `synthetic` \| REQUIRED \| True when the content is artificially generated or manipulated, in whole or in part |
| `key:5.4:AssetProvenance.voice_consent_ref` | `voice_consent_ref` \| OPTIONAL \| The consent of the person whose voice is synthesized |
| `key:5.4:CreativeDelivery.endpoint` | `endpoint` \| OPTIONAL \| Where the buyer sends creative |
| `key:5.4:CreativeDelivery.method` | `method` \| REQUIRED \| Core terms: `buyer_hosted` \| `seller_hosted` \| `api` \| `platform_managed`, defined in the creative path below |
| `key:5.4:CreativeDelivery.other_protocols[]` | `other_protocols[]` \| OPTIONAL \| How a creative that list has no code for is delivered. Core term: `javascript_tag`, a tag whose script renders the ad. A receiver that doesn't know a term treats only that term as unavailable |
| `key:5.4:CreativeDelivery.protocols[]` | `protocols[]` \| OPTIONAL \| Values from AdCOM 1.0 List: Creative Subtypes - Audio/Video the seller accepts, wrappers included, such as VAST 4.2 (13). The values OpenRTB 2.6 `video.protocols` and `audio.protocols` carry |
| `key:5.4:CreativeDelivery.secure_required` | `secure_required` \| OPTIONAL \| Whether the creative must load over a secure connection. Absent: this line item states no such requirement |
| `key:5.4:FormatSpec.spec` | `spec` \| REQUIRED \| The full specification. OpenProposal does not hardcode a shape per channel |
| `key:5.4:FormatSpec.spec_ref` | `spec_ref` \| OPTIONAL \| The format registry entry the spec comes from. Absent when the seller defines it |
| `key:5.4:MaterialsDue.at` | `at` \| REQUIRED when `before_start` is absent \| The deadline |
| `key:5.4:MaterialsDue.before_start` | `before_start` \| REQUIRED when `at` is absent \| The deadline, as a length of time before `commitment.flight.start` |
| `key:5.4:ProductionOption.built_by` | `built_by` \| OPTIONAL \| Which side builds it |
| `key:5.4:ProductionOption.custom_unit` | `custom_unit` \| OPTIONAL \| The custom unit built |
| `key:5.4:ProductionOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1) |
| `key:5.4:ProductionOption.lead_time` | `lead_time` \| OPTIONAL \| Time the build needs |
| `key:5.5:AcceptedExclusions.types` | `types` \| REQUIRED \| The exclusion types the seller accepts (core terms above), and its terms for each |
| `key:5.5:AcceptedExclusions.verification[]` | `verification[]` \| OPTIONAL \| The third parties that can attest the seller enforced these lists |
| `key:5.5:ClockWindow.before` | `before` \| REQUIRED \| The first minute after the window, later than `start` |
| `key:5.5:ClockWindow.days[]` | `days[]` \| REQUIRED \| The days the window repeats on |
| `key:5.5:ClockWindow.start` | `start` \| REQUIRED \| The first minute. Not `24:00` |
| `key:5.5:ExclusionTerms.enforcement` | `enforcement` \| REQUIRED \| `pre_serve` \| `best_effort` |
| `key:5.5:ExclusionTerms.max_entries` | `max_entries` \| OPTIONAL \| The most entries of this type the seller accepts |
| `key:5.5:FrequencyCap.count` | `count` \| REQUIRED \| The cap. The buyer's `value` is the count, and `period` and `per` stay the seller's |
| `key:5.5:FrequencyCap.per` | `per` \| REQUIRED \| Who is capped. It matters: CTV typically caps at household, audio at listener. Core terms: `device` \| `household` \| `listener` \| `screen` |
| `key:5.5:FrequencyCap.period` | `period` \| REQUIRED \| The bucket the count runs over |
| `key:5.6.1:Commit.entry` | `entry` \| REQUIRED \| The buyer's entry (§ 4.1.2), with `role: commitment` |
| `key:5.6.1:Commit.writes` | `writes` \| REQUIRED \| The buyer's writes (§ 3.4), keyed by each `line_item_id` that `entry.line_item_ids[]` names |
| `key:5.6.1:CommitResult.availability` | `availability` \| REQUIRED when `entries[]` holds a `decline` for lack of availability \| The figures that `decline` states, keyed by `line_item_id`. They are not part of the entry |
| `key:5.6.1:CommitResult.entries[]` | `entries[]` \| REQUIRED \| The buyer's `commitment` entry, then each matching `acceptance`, `decline`, or `withdrawal` entry written after it in `assent[]` |
| `key:5.6.1:Commitment.advertisers[]` | `advertisers[]` \| REQUIRED \| Whom the spend is for. It holds at least one entry |
| `key:5.6.1:Commitment.derived_units` | `derived_units` \| OPTIONAL \| The committed units, as § 5.6.5 derives them, whether or not this key is written. A written value is a copy, outside the record (§ 4.1.1); where it differs, the § 5.6.5 result applies |
| `key:5.6.1:Commitment.flight` | `flight` \| REQUIRED \| The buyer's campaign. Distinct from `offer_window` |
| `key:5.6.1:Commitment.pricing_id` | `pricing_id` \| REQUIRED when `pricing[]` has more than one entry \| The entry the buyer commits under |
| `key:5.6.1:CommitmentAmount.amount` | `amount` \| REQUIRED \| The figure committed: an `Amount` when `basis` is `spend`, otherwise a whole number of the entry's units (§ 5.6.5) |
| `key:5.6.1:CommitmentAmount.basis` | `basis` \| REQUIRED \| `spend` \| `units` |
| `key:5.6.1:CommitmentBounds.earliest_start` | `earliest_start` \| OPTIONAL \| Informative: the earliest `commitment.flight.start` the seller expects |
| `key:5.6.1:CommitmentBounds.latest_end` | `latest_end` \| OPTIONAL \| Informative: the latest `commitment.flight.end` the seller expects |
| `key:5.6.1:CommitmentBounds.spend` | `spend` \| OPTIONAL \| Bounds on the seller total (§ 5.6.5) |
| `key:5.6.2:Hold.held_availability` | `held_availability` \| OPTIONAL \| What the hold reserves, as the seller states it on a grant or a re-offer. It can be less than `requested_terms` asks |
| `key:5.6.2:Hold.position` | `position` \| OPTIONAL \| Seller-set, 1 for first in line, in the order granted. A later hold moves up only when the one ahead expires, is released or converts. Holds on the same inventory reserve it in that order, so a later hold reserves only what the holds granted ahead of it leave (§ 5.6.1) |
| `key:5.6.2:Hold.reference` | `reference` \| OPTIONAL \| Seller-set. The seller's id for the reservation, so a buyer can reconcile a hold against the seller's systems |
| `key:5.6.2:Hold.requested_terms` | `requested_terms` \| OPTIONAL \| The composition the buyer asks to hold, which is not a `commit`. A field it doesn't name reads as § 3.1 and § 3.2 put in effect |
| `key:5.6.2:Hold.status` | `status` \| REQUIRED \| Moved by the transitions below |
| `key:5.6.2:Hold.valid_until` | `valid_until` \| REQUIRED when `status` is `held` \| The grant time plus `hold_terms.duration`, or the § 4.3.4 cap if that comes first. With no `duration`, the seller sets it within the cap. It MAY remain on `expired` and `released` |
| `key:5.6.2:Hold.version` | `version` \| REQUIRED \| The `version` the hold is on (§ 4.1.3) |
| `key:5.6.2:HoldTerms.available` | `available` \| REQUIRED \| Whether this line item may be held |
| `key:5.6.2:HoldTerms.duration` | `duration` \| OPTIONAL \| How long a hold runs from its grant |
| `key:5.6.4:DeliveryCounting.impression_multiplier` | `impression_multiplier` \| REQUIRED when the line item counts impressions \| What turns a play into impressions. A line item that counts only plays needs none |
| `key:5.6.4:DeliveryCounting.partial_play_credit` | `partial_play_credit` \| REQUIRED when a property has `sync_groups[]` \| What a display on fewer than `min_screens_per_play` screens counts. Core terms: `none` (no play) \| `pro_rata` (screens played ÷ `min_screens_per_play`) |
| `key:5.6.4:DeliveryCounting.play_tolerance_ms` | `play_tolerance_ms` \| REQUIRED \| How far short of its full duration a play may end and still count |
| `key:5.6.4:DeliveryCounting.proof_of_performance` | `proof_of_performance` \| REQUIRED \| Who logs the plays and what the buyer receives |
| `key:5.6.4:ImpressionMultiplier.accreditation` | `accreditation` \| OPTIONAL \| The standing of the multiplier's source in the market. Core terms: `industry_currency` (the market's joint industry currency, such as Geopath in the US or Route in the UK) \| `publisher_reported` (the publisher's own figure). A receiver that doesn't know a term reads the multiplier without it |
| `key:5.6.4:ImpressionMultiplier.dataset` | `dataset` \| REQUIRED \| The source's named data release the multipliers come from. A new release is a new `dataset` |
| `key:5.6.4:ImpressionMultiplier.demographic` | `demographic` \| OPTIONAL \| The demographic whose value settles when `dataset` gives a value per demographic. The same kind of term as `cost_parameters.demographic` (§ 5.6.5) |
| `key:5.6.4:ImpressionMultiplier.impression_type` | `impression_type` \| REQUIRED \| `gross` \| `viewable` \| `lts` \| `audience`: Gross, Viewable (OTS), Likelihood-to-See, or Audience impressions, as the MRC Out-of-Home Measurement Standards (Phase 1 & 2 Combined, December 2025) define them |
| `key:5.6.4:ImpressionMultiplier.settles_on` | `settles_on` \| REQUIRED \| `declared` \| `restated`. `declared`: the value `dataset` gives that screen, or sync group, and hour when the play runs. Under a deal mechanism (§ 5.9), the bid request carries it as OpenRTB 2.6 `imp.qty.multiplier`. `restated`: that value after any reissue of `dataset` by its source before the count is final (§ 5.7.1) |
| `key:5.6.4:ImpressionMultiplier.source_type` | `source_type` \| REQUIRED \| Who supplies the multiplier. A value from AdCOM 1.0 List: DOOH Multiplier Measurement Source Types other than Unknown (`0`), such as Publisher Provided (`2`). The value OpenRTB 2.6 `imp.qty.sourcetype` carries |
| `key:5.6.4:ImpressionMultiplier.vendor` | `vendor` \| REQUIRED when `source_type` is not Publisher Provided (`2`) \| The domain of the vendor or exchange that supplies the multipliers, as OpenRTB 2.6 `imp.qty.vendor` names a vendor |
| `key:5.6.4:ProofOfPerformance.evidence[]` | `evidence[]` \| REQUIRED \| What the buyer receives. Core terms: `play_log` \| `photo` \| `audit_report` |
| `key:5.6.4:ProofOfPerformance.logged_by` | `logged_by` \| REQUIRED \| Who keeps the proof-of-play log |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.action_definition` | `action_definition` \| OPTIONAL \| Absent on a core term other than `CPA`. The rules that make an action billable: the evidence that confirms it, which content or context gets credit, and how cancellations, returns, fraud, and duplicate reports change the count. Where the document conflicts with a typed field of the binding record (§ 4.1.1), such as `action_type`, `attribution_window`, or `action_source`, the typed field governs |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.action_source` | `action_source` \| REQUIRED when `cost_method` is `CPA` \| Who reports the action |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.action_type` | `action_type` \| REQUIRED when `cost_method` is `CPA` \| The kind of action counted. Core terms in IAB Tech Lab ECAPI 1.0: its `event_type` values, from the Standard Events and Additional Events tables both, other than `custom`, such as `purchase` or `install`. Any other action is an extension term, such as `shop.example:store_visit` |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.attribution_window` | `attribution_window` \| REQUIRED when `cost_method` is `CPA` \| How long after exposure an action counts |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.calendar_zone` | `calendar_zone` \| OPTIONAL \| Absent unless `rate_unit` is `day`. The zone whose calendar days the rate buys: each calendar day in that zone that the flight touches is one unit. The flight's `end` is its last second, inclusive (§ 2.2), so a flight to the end of a day ends at 23:59:59 in that zone, and an `end` at the next midnight adds that day |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.demographic` | `demographic` \| REQUIRED when `cost_method` is `CPP` \| The rated demographic, such as `ratings.example:adults_25_54`. Its definition states whether co-viewers and impressions of unknown age count, or says `unknown`. This version lists no core terms |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.rate_unit` | `rate_unit` \| REQUIRED when `cost_method` is `TIME` \| `hour` \| `day` \| `week`. The unit the rate buys, counted in elapsed units unless `calendar_zone` is present |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.rating_source` | `rating_source` \| REQUIRED when `cost_method` is `CPP` \| Who supplies the ratings |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.view_fraction` | `view_fraction` \| REQUIRED when `cost_method` is `CPV` and `view_s` is absent \| The fraction of the creative played that makes a view. Absent when `view_s` is present |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.view_s` | `view_s` \| REQUIRED when `cost_method` is `CPV` and `view_fraction` is absent \| Seconds played that make a view. Absent when `view_fraction` is present |
| `key:5.6.5:CostParameters.viewability_standard` | `viewability_standard` \| REQUIRED when `cost_method` is `CPMV` \| The viewability standard that counts a unit. Core term: `mrc`, the MRC guidelines that `viewable_rate` cites (§ 5.7.1) |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationNotice.cancelled_share` | `cancelled_share` \| REQUIRED \| The share of the remaining commitment cancelled, up to the `max_share` that applies |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationNotice.notice_received_at` | `notice_received_at` \| REQUIRED \| When the seller received notice |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationNotice.option_id` | `option_id` \| REQUIRED \| The `id` of the option in effect that the notice exercises |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationNotice.requested_effective_at` | `requested_effective_at` \| REQUIRED \| The effective date the buyer asks for |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationOption.dated_shares[]` | `dated_shares[]` \| OPTIONAL \| Dates with their own `max_share`. Outside every entry's `window`, the top-level `max_share` applies |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationOption.fees[]` | `fees[]` \| REQUIRED \| The fee, by how long before the reference date notice arrives. MUST be non-empty |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1) |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationOption.max_share` | `max_share` \| REQUIRED \| Cancellable share of the remaining commitment. `0`: non-cancellable |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationOption.notice_duration` | `notice_duration` \| REQUIRED \| Time from notice received to the earliest effective date: `P14D` is 14 days, `P0D` immediate |
| `key:5.6.6:CancellationOption.notice_reference` | `notice_reference` \| OPTIONAL \| The date `before_reference` counts to. Core terms: `flight` (default) \| `install_date`. `flight`: `commitment.flight.start`. `install_date`: the posting or install date. No key in the record holds that date, so a fee that counts to it can't be computed from the record |
| `key:5.6.6:DatedShare.max_share` | `max_share` \| REQUIRED \| Cancellable share of the remaining commitment when this entry applies |
| `key:5.6.6:DatedShare.window` | `window` \| REQUIRED \| The dates it covers. An option's windows don't overlap |
| `key:5.6.6:Fee.amount` | `amount` \| REQUIRED when `calculation` is `fixed` \| The fee as money |
| `key:5.6.6:Fee.before_reference` | `before_reference` \| OPTIONAL \| Default `P0D`. Measured from notice received to the `notice_reference` date, or to the `window.start` of the `dated_shares[]` entry that applies. The entry with the longest `before_reference` the notice meets applies; notice that meets none takes the shortest |
| `key:5.6.6:Fee.calculation` | `calculation` \| REQUIRED \| `none` \| `cancelled` \| `commitment` \| `fixed`. `none`: no fee. `cancelled`: `share` of the cancelled amount. `commitment`: `share` of `commitment.amount` without `delta_flat` charges, whatever has been delivered. `fixed`: `amount` |
| `key:5.6.6:Fee.share` | `share` \| REQUIRED when `calculation` is `cancelled` or `commitment` \| The fee as a share |
| `key:5.6.7:ExclusivityOption.categories` | `categories` \| REQUIRED when `scope` is `category` \| `taxonomy` per AdCOM 1.0 List: Category Taxonomies. An id covers its descendants |
| `key:5.6.7:ExclusivityOption.competitors[]` | `competitors[]` \| OPTIONAL \| Advertiser domains also excluded |
| `key:5.6.7:ExclusivityOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1) |
| `key:5.6.7:ExclusivityOption.scope` | `scope` \| REQUIRED \| `category` \| `sole`. `category`: no other advertiser in `categories`. `sole`: no other advertiser |
| `key:5.6.7:ExclusivityOption.separation` | `separation` \| REQUIRED \| Where the term holds. Core terms: `inventory` (all of this line item) \| `page` (each page) \| `pod` (each ad pod) \| `stream` (each content stream an ad server fills) \| `episode` (each podcast episode) \| `loop` (each DOOH screen's loop) \| `location` (each venue or site of this line item's properties, static or digital) \| `paid_zone` (the part of each venue or site of this line item's properties past its paid entry, such as inside a station's fare gates) |
| `key:5.6.7:ExclusivityOption.window` | `window` \| OPTIONAL \| The dates the term holds, only where they fall inside `commitment.flight`. Default: the whole flight. With no overlap, the term holds on no date and the option's deltas still count (§ 5.6) |
| `key:5.6.7:Preemption.notice_duration` | `notice_duration` \| OPTIONAL \| The least time from the seller's notice to the first preempted delivery. Absent: no notice is owed |
| `key:5.6.7:Preemption.reason` | `reason` \| REQUIRED \| What can preempt this line item. Core term: `exclusivity`, an exclusivity term the seller accepts later that holds, under its `separation` and `window`, where and when this line item delivers |
| `key:5.6.7:Preemption.remedy` | `remedy` \| OPTIONAL \| What the buyer gets for the delivery a preemption takes, on any `delivery_type`. Core terms: `makegood` \| `credit` \| `none`, where `none` gives nothing for that delivery only. Absent: `shortfall_remedy` (§ 5.7) settles any shortfall the preemption causes |
| `key:5.6:Availability.certainty` | `certainty` \| REQUIRED \| `forecast` \| `reservable`. `forecast`: the seller's estimate of the quantity. `reservable`: the quantity the seller says it can reserve for `window`. MUST be `forecast` when the line item's `delivery_type` (§ 5.9) is `non_guaranteed` |
| `key:5.6:Availability.computed_at` | `computed_at` \| REQUIRED \| When the seller computed it |
| `key:5.6:Availability.granularity` | `granularity` \| OPTIONAL \| The bucket `quantity` counts per, within `window`. Absent: `quantity` is the total over `window` |
| `key:5.6:Availability.quantity` | `quantity` \| REQUIRED \| How many. It can be fractional where the unit is, such as `rating_points` per `day` |
| `key:5.6:Availability.unit` | `unit` \| REQUIRED \| What `quantity` counts. Core terms: the `unit` terms of § 5.6.5, and `slots_per_loop` (as the § 5.2.4 key) \| `unit_periods` (one unit for one period, such as a board for a 4-week cycle). A receiver that doesn't know a term can't compare the figure, and still reads the line item |
| `key:5.6:Availability.window` | `window` \| REQUIRED \| The dates `quantity` covers. On a read (§ 3.3) or a decline (§ 5.6.1), the flight the buyer sent |
| `key:5.7.1:CommittedMetric.direction` | `direction` \| REQUIRED \| `at_least` \| `at_most` |
| `key:5.7.1:CommittedMetric.level` | `level` \| REQUIRED \| The offered level: a `Fraction` under a rate term, a count under a count term |
| `key:5.7.1:CommittedMetric.metric` | `metric` \| REQUIRED \| What is measured. Core terms below. A receiver that doesn't know the term skips the line item (§ 2.2): a tuple binds outside any option |
| `key:5.7.1:ControllingMeasurementOption.counted_by` | `counted_by` \| REQUIRED \| Who counts. When the `pricing[]` entry the buyer commits under has `cost_method` `CPP`, `counted_by` MUST be its `cost_parameters.rating_source`. When it has `cost_method` `CPA`, `counted_by` MUST be its `cost_parameters.action_source`. Otherwise, when `channel` is `dooh` and the line item counts impressions, `counted_by` MUST be the source of the line item's `delivery_counting.impression_multiplier` |
| `key:5.7.1:ControllingMeasurementOption.discrepancy_threshold` | `discrepancy_threshold` \| OPTIONAL \| A fraction of the controlling count. When the other measurement, a party's own count of the same units, exceeds the controlling count by more than this over an invoice period, reconciliation opens |
| `key:5.7.1:ControllingMeasurementOption.final_after` | `final_after` \| OPTIONAL \| Such as `P30D` (30 days). A count is final that long after its invoice period ends. Committed values, whichever source measures them, are final that long after `commitment.flight.end` |
| `key:5.7.1:ControllingMeasurementOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1) |
| `key:5.7.1:ControllingMeasurementOption.qualifier` | `qualifier` \| OPTIONAL \| A product or method of `counted_by`, such as a named ad server |
| `key:5.7.1:MeasurementOption.attribution_window` | `attribution_window` \| OPTIONAL \| How long after exposure the provider attributes an event |
| `key:5.7.1:MeasurementOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1) |
| `key:5.7.1:MeasurementOption.measured_by` | `measured_by` \| REQUIRED \| The measurement provider |
| `key:5.7.1:MeasurementOption.qualifier` | `qualifier` \| OPTIONAL \| A product or method of `measured_by` |
| `key:5.7.1:RemedyTerms.makegood_type` | `makegood_type` \| OPTIONAL \| What a makegood delivers. Core terms: `same_unit` (more delivery on the inventory bought) \| `comparable_unit` (delivery on other inventory) \| `added_time` (more running time) |
| `key:5.7.1:RemedyTerms.makegood_window` | `makegood_window` \| OPTIONAL \| How long past `commitment.flight.end` a makegood may run |
| `key:5.7.1:ReportingOption.cadence` | `cadence` \| OPTIONAL \| How often a report arrives |
| `key:5.7.1:ReportingOption.delivery_method` | `delivery_method` \| OPTIONAL \| How a report arrives. Core terms: `api` \| `s3` |
| `key:5.7.1:ReportingOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| Option id (§ 3.1) |
| `key:5.7.1:ReportingOption.latency` | `latency` \| OPTIONAL \| How long after the period a report arrives |
| `key:5.7.1:ReportingOption.provided_metrics[]` | `provided_metrics[]` \| OPTIONAL \| The metrics a report holds. Core terms: the `unit` terms of § 5.6.5, and the Rate terms of the `metric` table (§ 5.7.1) |
| `key:5.7.1:ReportingOption.report_type` | `report_type` \| REQUIRED \| The report's grain. Core terms: `log_level` (one record per event) \| `aggregate` (totals over a period or a dimension) |
| `key:5.7.1:ShortfallRemedy.remedy_terms` | `remedy_terms` \| OPTIONAL \| The seller's terms for a remedy |
| `key:5.8:RequiredCredential.credential_type` | `credential_type` \| REQUIRED \| What the buyer holds. Core term: `iab_registry`, an entry in the IAB Tech Lab Agent Registry |
| `key:5.8:RequiredCredential.issuer` | `issuer` \| REQUIRED \| Who issues it |
| `key:5.9:BuyerRequirements.allowed_dsps[]` | `allowed_dsps[]` \| OPTIONAL \| The DSPs that may receive the deal (§ 5.8), each by its primary domain, as `SeatRef.dsp` (§ 2.2) |
| `key:5.9:BuyerRequirements.seat_owner` | `seat_owner` \| OPTIONAL \| The legal entity that owns the seats. Absent: the `buyer` |
| `key:5.9:BuyerRequirements.seats` | `seats` \| OPTIONAL \| The buyer's seats on the deal, as the Deals API 1.0 `wseat` |
| `key:5.9:BuyerRequirements.seats_expected` | `seats_expected` \| OPTIONAL \| The seller asks the buyer to supply `seats` |
| `key:5.9:MechanismOption.delivery_type` | `delivery_type` \| REQUIRED \| What the seller promises under this mechanism. The value on the mechanism in effect (§ 3.1) applies to the line item |
| `key:5.9:MechanismOption.id` | `id` \| REQUIRED \| The mechanism, which is the option id (§ 3.1) |

#### definition (79)

| ID | Text |
| :--- | :--- |
| `def:1.1:transaction_mechanism.direct_io` | `direct_io` \| OpenDirect 2.1 · Order + Line \| `order`, `line` \| An OpenDirect 2.1 ChangeRequest on the booked Line (`lineid`). `change_requests[]` (§ 4.4) records it against the baseline `VersionRef` (§ 2.2). A partial cancel uses ChangeRequestLine, and a full cancel uses Line `?cancel` at the effective date |
| `def:1.1:transaction_mechanism.preferred_deal` | `preferred_deal` \| Deals API 1.0 · `Deal` + OpenRTB 2.6 · `Deal` \| `deal` \| By agreement between implementers, against the same baseline. A full stop is `sellerstatus` 4 (archived) |
| `def:1.1:transaction_mechanism.private_auction` | `private_auction` \| Deals API 1.0 · `Deal` + OpenRTB 2.6 · `Deal` \| `deal` \| As for `preferred_deal` |
| `def:1.1:transaction_mechanism.programmatic_guaranteed` | `programmatic_guaranteed` \| OpenDirect 2.1 · Order + Line, as the reservation; Deals API 1.0 · `Deal` \| `order`, `line`, `deal` \| The reservation: as for `direct_io`. The deal: as for `preferred_deal` |
| `def:2.2:Amount` | `Amount` \| Number within the limits of § 4.1.1 \| Money. It carries no currency: § 5.6 states which one it reads in |
| `def:2.2:ApprovalStatus` | `ApprovalStatus` \| `PENDING` \| `APPROVED` \| `REJECTED` \| An approval outcome, in the spelling of OpenDirect 2.1's ChangeRequest `status` |
| `def:2.2:CalendarUnit` | `CalendarUnit` \| `hour` \| `day` \| `week` \| `month` \| A calendar bucket |
| `def:2.2:Digest` | `Digest` \| String of 71 characters: `sha256:` followed by the 64 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 of its input \| The value carries the algorithm, and a field name never names it. `sha256` is the only algorithm in this version. Each field that holds a `Digest` states its input |
| `def:2.2:Domain` | `Domain` \| String: lowercase ASCII, internationalized labels in A-label form (RFC 5890), with no scheme, port, path, or trailing dot \| Producers MUST write this form. Consumers MUST compare byte for byte and MUST NOT normalize a value before comparing it. A value read from ads.txt or sellers.json is converted to this form before comparison |
| `def:2.2:Duration` | `Duration` \| String: the ISO 8601 grammar of RFC 3339 Appendix A without its week form (`P14D`, not `P2W`) \| A length of time. A comparison names its anchor. A length within the playback of an ad or a placement, such as a play, view, read, loop, slot, or refresh, is instead an Integer whose key ends `_s` or `_ms`, except a key imported from another standard, which keeps its spelling (§ 2.6) |
| `def:2.2:EntryRef` | `EntryRef` \| String: `<domain>/<collection>/<entry_id>@<version>`, with no `/` or `@` inside a part \| A named entry at one exact version, whose content does not change. `<domain>` is a `Domain`. It carries no digest |
| `def:2.2:Ext` | `Ext` \| `Map[Domain, Object]` \| The extension container. § 2.5 states its rules |
| `def:2.2:FieldPointer` | `FieldPointer` \| String: a JSON Pointer (RFC 6901) \| A location in the object this specification defines |
| `def:2.2:Fraction` | `Fraction` \| Number from 0 to 1 \| A proportion |
| `def:2.2:FractionDelta` | `FractionDelta` \| Number greater than -1 \| Used only by `delta_fraction` (§ 3.1) |
| `def:2.2:HttpsUrl` | `HttpsUrl` \| A `Url` whose scheme is `https` \| A location fetched over TLS |
| `def:2.2:IanaZone` | `IanaZone` \| String: a name in the IANA time zone database, such as `America/New_York` or `UTC` \| A time zone |
| `def:2.2:Instant` | `Instant` \| String: an RFC 3339 `date-time` in whole seconds, ending in `Z` \| A point in time. One written form, so one instant has one digest input |
| `def:2.2:IntegerFlag` | `IntegerFlag` \| Integer: `0` (no) \| `1` (yes) \| A yes or no that another standard writes as an integer. Each field says what `1` affirms |
| `def:2.2:LineItemStatus` | `LineItemStatus` \| A `ProposalStatus`, or `sold_out` \| A line item's `status`, moved as § 4.3.2 states |
| `def:2.2:Measurer` | `Measurer` \| A `Side`, or a `RegistrableDomain` \| Who counts or measures. A seller writes `seller`, never its own domain. Two values name one party only when they are equal byte for byte. A `qualifier` beside a value names a product or method of that party and is not compared |
| `def:2.2:ProposalStatus` | `ProposalStatus` \| `draft` \| `published` \| `under_review` \| `agreed` \| `withdrawn` \| `expired` \| A proposal's `status`, computed by § 4.3.3 |
| `def:2.2:RegistrableDomain` | `RegistrableDomain` \| A `Domain` that is the Public Suffix List registrable domain, taken over the whole list, PRIVATE section included \| It names a business |
| `def:2.2:Side` | `Side` \| `buyer` \| `seller` \| It says which side, never who that side is. `Party` says who |
| `def:2.2:TimeUnit` | `TimeUnit` \| A `CalendarUnit`, or `flight` \| `flight` is the whole of `commitment.flight` |
| `def:2.2:Url` | `Url` \| String: an absolute `http` or `https` URL (RFC 3986) \| A location a client can fetch |
| `def:2.2:VocabularyTerm` | `VocabularyTerm` \| String: a bare core term, or `<domain>:<term>` with `<domain>` a `Domain` the definer controls. A term is ASCII letters, digits, `_`, and `-` \| See below |
| `def:2.2:ZoneName` | `ZoneName` \| An `IanaZone`, or `viewer_local` \| `viewer_local` is where the ad renders. On a `dooh` line item that is each screen's own zone, which its `time_zone` names when present |
| `def:2.4:code.expired` | `expired` \| `offer_window.end` or `price_valid_until` has passed |
| `def:2.4:code.idempotency_conflict` | `idempotency_conflict` \| An `idempotency_key` is reused with a different `Commit` (§ 5.6.1) |
| `def:2.4:code.not_committable` | `not_committable` \| A line item's `status` rules out an ask (§ 4.3.2), a `commit` names part of a package or an add-on without its package (§ 4.1.4), a live `commitment` entry already names a line item (§ 4.1.2), or a seller rejects a `commit` before `offer_window.start` (§ 5.6.1) |
| `def:2.4:code.not_eligible` | `not_eligible` \| The buyer or the sender fails an eligibility or authority check the seller applies (§ 5.8) |
| `def:2.4:code.not_found` | `not_found` \| A named proposal or line item does not exist, or the requester may not read it (§ 5.8) |
| `def:2.4:code.not_offered` | `not_offered` \| A buyer value is outside what the seller declared (§ 3.1, § 3.2, § 4.3.2, § 5.3, § 5.5, § 5.6, § 5.6.1), or a buyer asks for a hold where `hold_terms` offers none (§ 5.6.2) |
| `def:2.4:code.record_mismatch` | `record_mismatch` \| A `record_digest` in the request does not equal the one the receiver recomputes, and § 5.6.1 gives no other code (§ 4.1.1) |
| `def:2.4:code.stale_version` | `stale_version` \| An ask is stale (§ 4.1.3) |
| `def:2.4:code.unresolved_reference` | `unresolved_reference` \| A `DocumentRef` (§ 2.2) does not resolve, or its content does not match its `digest` |
| `def:2.4:code.validation` | `validation` \| A value breaks a rule of this specification and no other code applies |
| `def:5.1.1:channel.audio` | `audio` \| Audio ad (`AudioPlacement`) |
| `def:5.1.1:channel.ctv` | `ctv` \| A TV device type |
| `def:5.1.1:channel.display` | `display` \| Any other display ad (`DisplayPlacement`) |
| `def:5.1.1:channel.dooh` | `dooh` \| Distribution channel `Dooh` |
| `def:5.1.1:channel.native` | `native` \| Native ad (`DisplayPlacement.nativefmt`), including native ads with video assets |
| `def:5.1.1:channel.video` | `video` \| Video ad (`VideoPlacement`) |
| `def:5.2.1:environments.app_mobile` | `app_mobile` \| `App` \| The mobile device types |
| `def:5.2.1:environments.ctv` | `ctv` \| `Site` or `App` \| The TV device types |
| `def:5.2.1:environments.dooh` | `dooh` \| `Dooh` \| Any |
| `def:5.2.1:environments.web_desktop` | `web_desktop` \| `Site` \| Personal Computer (2) |
| `def:5.2.1:environments.web_mobile` | `web_mobile` \| `Site` \| The mobile device types |
| `def:5.2.6:seller_relationship.inventory_partner` | `inventory_partner` \| `INVENTORYPARTNERDOMAIN` equal to `seller.domain` |
| `def:5.2.6:seller_relationship.manager` | `manager` \| `MANAGERDOMAIN` equal to `seller.domain`, global or for every country the line item's `geo` or `markets` allows |
| `def:5.2.6:seller_relationship.owner` | `owner` \| `OWNERDOMAIN` equal to `seller.domain`. With no `OWNERDOMAIN`: the domain serving the file equal to `seller.domain`, or a `DIRECT` record for a `seller_accounts[]` entry that sellers.json types `PUBLISHER` or `BOTH` |
| `def:5.2.6:seller_relationship.reseller` | `reseller` \| A `RESELLER` record for a `seller_accounts[]` entry |
| `def:5.4:method.api` | `api` \| The seller \| The creative, through the seller's API, at `endpoint` when the seller names one. `assets[]` records it |
| `def:5.4:method.buyer_hosted` | `buyer_hosted` \| The buyer's ad server \| A URL or tag, in `assets[]`. When the bid carries the tag, nothing |
| `def:5.4:method.platform_managed` | `platform_managed` \| The platform that runs the buy, such as the DSP \| Nothing. The platform holds the creative and its review |
| `def:5.4:method.seller_hosted` | `seller_hosted` \| The seller \| The creative, in `assets[]` |
| `def:5.5:exclusions.apps` | `apps` \| `AppId` \| An app (§ 5.2.5). A seller MUST reject an entry whose `store` it doesn't know, rather than apply it across stores, with `validation` |
| `def:5.5:exclusions.categories` | `categories` \| `TaxonomyCodes` \| `taxonomy` per AdCOM 1.0 List: Category Taxonomies. An id covers only itself, not its descendants |
| `def:5.5:exclusions.domains` | `domains` \| `Domain` \| A domain. It covers only itself, not its subdomains |
| `def:5.5:exclusions.keywords` | `keywords` \| `String` \| A keyword |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.CPA` | `CPA` \| `actions` \| Action \| 1 \| `action_type`, `attribution_window`, `action_source` |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.CPC` | `CPC` \| `clicks` \| Click \| 1 |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.CPCV` | `CPCV` \| `completed_views` \| Completed view: 100% of the creative played \| 1 |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.CPM` | `CPM` \| `impressions` \| Impression \| 1,000 |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.CPMV` | `CPMV` \| `viewable_impressions` \| Viewable impression \| 1,000 \| `viewability_standard` |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.CPP` | `CPP` \| `rating_points` \| Rating point \| 1 \| `demographic`, `rating_source` |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.CPV` | `CPV` \| `views` \| View \| 1 \| `view_s` or `view_fraction` |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.FLAT` | `FLAT` \| The line item |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.PLAY` | `PLAY` \| `plays` \| DOOH play on one screen or one sync group \| 1,000 |
| `def:5.6.5:cost_method.TIME` | `TIME` \| `hours` \| `days` \| `weeks`, by `rate_unit` \| One `rate_unit` \| 1 \| `rate_unit` |
| `def:5.7.1:metric.completion_rate` | `completion_rate` \| Rate \| `at_least` \| Completed views per started ad, as the VAST `complete` and `start` tracking events report them. Usual on video, audio, and CTV |
| `def:5.7.1:metric.impressions` | `impressions` \| Count \| `at_least` \| Impressions on a `dooh` line item, counted under `delivery_counting` (§ 5.6.4) |
| `def:5.7.1:metric.ivt_rate` | `ivt_rate` \| Rate \| `at_most` \| Impressions identified as invalid traffic per measured impression, with invalid traffic as the MRC Invalid Traffic Detection and Filtration Standards Addendum (IVT 2.0) defines it |
| `def:5.7.1:metric.viewable_rate` | `viewable_rate` \| Rate \| `at_least` \| Viewable impressions per measured impression, as the MRC Viewable Ad Impression Measurement Guidelines (Desktop, Version 2.0) and the MRC Mobile Viewable Ad Impression Measurement Guidelines (June 2016) define them. Usual on display |
| `def:5.9:transaction_mechanism.direct_io` | `direct_io` \| No \| Any |
| `def:5.9:transaction_mechanism.preferred_deal` | `preferred_deal` \| Yes \| `non_guaranteed` |
| `def:5.9:transaction_mechanism.private_auction` | `private_auction` \| Yes \| `non_guaranteed` |
| `def:5.9:transaction_mechanism.programmatic_guaranteed` | `programmatic_guaranteed` \| Yes \| `guaranteed`, `share_of_voice` |

#### marker (4)

| ID | Text |
| :--- | :--- |
| `marker:3:asserted` | `asserted` \| The seller states it from data the buyer can't recompute. Not a term, and it changes without a new version. |
| `marker:3:lifecycle` | `lifecycle` \| Live state of the object. The party its section names moves it between the stated values. Not a term. |
| `marker:3:mixed` | `mixed` \| The keys of the object carry different markers: in the Mutability column of its key table, in its lead-in for composed keys, or on its rows for `pricing[]`. |
| `marker:3:recorded` | `recorded` \| Written by the party that acted. Append-only: an entry is never edited or removed. Not a term. |

| ID | Text |
| :--- | :--- |
| `concept:1.1:10` | `terms.countries` \| The countries of `geo` or `markets` in effect, in alpha-3 (below), without a country excluded whole. Unset when the line item declares neither |

| `concept:1.1:12` | `terms.cur` \| `Deal.bidfloorcur` \| That entry's `currency`, always sent |

| `concept:1.1:15` | `terms.units` \| `commitment.amount` when `basis` is `units`, otherwise `derived_units` (§ 5.6.5) |
| `concept:1.1:16` | `terms.totalcost` \| The seller total (§ 5.6.5) |

| `concept:1.1:3.1` | `Order.currency` \| The committed `pricing[]` entry's `currency` |
| `concept:1.1:3.2` | `Line.startdate`, `Line.enddate` \| `commitment.flight.start`, `commitment.flight.end` |
| `concept:1.1:3.3` | `Line.ratetype` \| That entry's `cost_method`: `CPM`, `CPMV`, and `CPC` as written, `FLAT` as `FlatRate`, and `TIME` with `rate_unit: day` as `CPD` |
| `concept:1.1:3.4` | `Line.rate` \| That entry's `agreed_rate` |
| `concept:1.1:3.5` | `Line.qty` \| `commitment.amount` when `basis` is `units`, `1` for `FLAT`, otherwise `derived_units` (§ 5.6.5) |
| `concept:1.1:3.6` | `Line.cost` \| The seller total (§ 5.6.5) |
| `concept:1.1:3.7` | `Line.frequencycount`, `Line.frequencyinterval` \| `frequency_cap.count` in effect, and `frequency_cap.period` in OpenDirect's spelling, with `flight` as `LineDuration` |
| `concept:1.1:5` | `adtypes` \| From `channel`: `display` 1, `video` 2, `audio` 3, `native` 4, and `ctv` 2 when `placement.plcmt` is present. Otherwise unset: the record doesn't state the ad's type |
| `concept:1.1:6` | `pubcount` \| On a `dooh` line item, `1` when the properties in effect belong to one inventory owner and `2` when they belong to more. `domains[]` names each owner (§ 5.2.4). Otherwise unset |
| `concept:1.1:7` | `dinventory` \| On a `dooh` line item, `1` when at least one property is in effect and each property in effect lists its `screens[]`. Otherwise unset |
| `concept:1.1:8` | `inventory.inclinventory` \| `[3]` when `dinventory` is `1`. Otherwise unset |
| `concept:1.1:9` | `terms.startdate`, `terms.enddate` \| `commitment.flight.start`, `commitment.flight.end` |
| `concept:1.2:1` | Message authentication \| Which origin sent this request or response? \| An HTTP Message Signature, with the key in that origin's Web Bot Auth directory \| This specification's Web Bot Auth profile (§ 2.8) \| Nothing. The object never carries or re-proves it |
| `concept:1.2:2` | Authorization \| What may this party, or its agent, do? \| The seller's eligibility rules; a mandate the principal publishes or signs \| This specification for the rules (§ 5.8); the AAMP authority document (provisional name) for mandates \| The rules only, never the proof |
| `concept:1.2:3` | Legal assent \| Which legal entity agreed to which bytes? \| Two matching `assent[]` entries over one `record_digest`, with both parties named in the record; as optional evidence, the signed messages that carried them \| This specification for the record, digest, entry, and what each party keeps (§ 4.1.1, § 4.1.2); § 2.8 for how a message is signed and its key found \| Parties, digest, entries |
| `concept:2.8.2:1` | Tag \| `tag="web-bot-auth"` \| `tag="web-bot-auth"` |
| `concept:2.8.2:2` | Count \| Exactly one `web-bot-auth` member in `Signature-Input`, and exactly one `Signature-Agent` member, keyed to its label. A verifier doesn't verify members with other tags, but every member must be well formed (RFC 9421 §§ 4.1–4.2) \| Same |
| `concept:2.8.2:3` | Components \| `@method`, `@target-uri`, `@authority`, `"signature-agent";key=<label>` \| `@status`, `"@method";req`, `"@target-uri";req`, `"signature-agent";key=<label>`, and, when the request carried a `web-bot-auth` signature, `"signature";req;key=<request label>` |
| `concept:2.8.2:4` | Body \| With a body, also `content-type` and `content-digest`. `Content-Digest` (RFC 9530) uses `sha-256`, and a verifier checks it against the body bytes \| Same |
| `concept:2.8.2:5` | Parameters \| `created`; `expires`, at most 300 seconds after `created`; `keyid`, the key's thumbprint; `alg="ed25519"`; and `nonce`, 64 random bytes as unpadded base64url or padded base64 \| The same, without `nonce` |
| `concept:2.8.2:6` | Clock \| A verifier allows 60 seconds of clock skew \| Same |
| `concept:2.8.6:1` | `400` \| The signature fields don't parse \| Section 5.4 |
| `concept:2.8.6:2` | `403` \| The signature fails verification or a rule of this profile, or a party that requires a signature gets none \| Section 5.3 |
| `concept:2.8.6:3` | `429` \| The `nonce` was used before. Sign again with a new one \| Section 5.3; RFC 9421 § 7.2.2 |
| `concept:2.8.6:4` | `503` \| The verifier can't check and record the `nonce`. Retry later, as `Retry-After` says, with the same or a new signature \| Appendix C.6 |
| `concept:4.3.1:1` | `publish` \| seller \| version 1 of a new or `draft` proposal, or the next `version` for a change to offered terms (§ 4.1.3); `published`. Moving a `draft` line item in a frozen version: neither (§ 4.3.2) |
| `concept:4.3.1:10` | hold request, grant, decline, release, re-offer \| buyer; seller; seller; buyer, or seller on a revision (rule 2); seller on a revision (rule 2) \| `hold` only; no entry |
| `concept:4.3.1:2` | availability read \| buyer \| an `AvailabilityRead` (§ 3.3), answered with the figures § 3.3 names, with no change of state; no entry |
| `concept:4.3.1:3` | `request` \| buyer \| asks a `requestable` value on one line item against a named `version`, stated as a `HistoryPath` (§ 4.1) with `requested_value`; `requested` |
| `concept:4.3.1:4` | `grant` \| seller \| of a `request`: new `version` with the requested value in force as an offered term; `granted` |
| `concept:4.3.1:5` | `counter` \| seller \| of a `request`: new `version` with another value; `countered`. A `counter` of `pricing[].list_rate` MAY also change `included[]`, with `paths[]` naming both |
| `concept:4.3.1:6` | `decline` \| seller \| no new `version`. Of a `request`: `declined`. Of a `commit`: the seller's `assent[]` entry; no entry |
| `concept:4.3.1:7` | `commit` \| buyer \| a `Commit` (§ 5.6.1): the buyer's `assent[]` entry, which pins the `version`, and its writes (§ 3.4); no entry |
| `concept:4.3.1:8` | `accept` \| seller \| of a `commit`: the seller's `assent[]` entry, no new `version`; no entry |
| `concept:4.3.1:9` | `withdraw` \| seller: line item or proposal; buyer: its own ask \| `withdrawn`. A seller's is one entry. For each line item it moved to `withdrawn`, its `paths[]` holds a `HistoryPath` with that `line_item_id` and the `pointer` `/status`. Of the buyer's `commit`: the buyer's `assent[]` entry; no entry |
| `concept:4.3.4:1` | `offer_window.end` \| the offer window \| every `published`, `under_review`, or `sold_out` line item is `expired`; open asks are void |
| `concept:4.3.4:2` | `pricing[].price_valid_until` \| the firm entry \| `status` unchanged; a later `commit` under that entry is rejected (§ 5.6.1), and the seller SHOULD `publish` or `withdraw` |
| `concept:4.3.4:3` | `hold.valid_until` \| the hold \| `hold.status` becomes `expired`; `status` unchanged |

| `rule:1.1:d539a004` | OPTIONAL. |
| `rule:2.1:373dbf11` | REQUIRED |
| `rule:2.1:373dbf11-2` | REQUIRED |
| `rule:2.1:5072813c` | A manifest entry holds it (§ 2.3), and a response over the message binding (§ 1.2) MAY carry it. |
| `rule:2.1:568d4699` | A full fetch MUST return it, except for line items and packages the requester may not read (§ 5.8). |
| `rule:2.1:57273714` | REQUIRED when the full form holds `line_items[]` |
| `rule:2.1:ae26d4ac` | OPTIONAL |
| `rule:2.1:ae26d4ac-2` | OPTIONAL |
| `rule:2.1:c0741f43` | REQUIRED when the full form holds `packages[]` |
| `rule:2.2:1e8acd47` | Consumers MUST compare byte for byte and MUST NOT normalize a value before comparing it. |

| `rule:2.5:54a97cce` | A seller's own entries MUST use its `seller.domain` as the key. |
| `rule:2.5:7bd50d5a` | It MUST NOT keep a line item after removing fields from it. |
| `rule:2.5:7eb6b4bc` | It MUST keep `ext` unchanged when it stores or forwards the document. |
| `rule:2.5:84b68ec7` | A term the counterparty must understand to transact MUST NOT be an `ext` entry; it is a field or a vocabulary term. |
| `rule:2.5:95586079` | Each key MUST be a `Domain` that its owner controls, and each value is an object that the owner defines. |
| `rule:2.5:b7667a2c` | The proposal, each line item, and each `change_requests[]` entry (§ 4.4) MAY carry `ext`, an `Ext`. |
| `rule:2.5:c97afe58` | Apart from an unknown term (§ 2.2), a receiver MAY skip a whole line item it cannot read, without assenting to it. |
| `rule:2.5:d8749eb6` | A receiver MUST ignore an `ext` entry it does not understand, and MUST NOT reject a document because of it. |
| `rule:2.5:f02c39d6` | An `ext` entry MUST NOT change the meaning of a field this specification defines. |
| `rule:2.6:6f3c4fcc` | A key table has the columns Key, Type, Required, and Meaning, and MAY add a last column that names a source standard. |
| `rule:2.8.5:d717d522` | A verifier MUST reject a `nonce` it has already seen from the same origin within the signature's lifetime, with `429`. |
| `rule:2.8.6:0f9375d8` | A requester that can't verify a response MAY refuse to write its own entry (§ 4.1.2). |
| `rule:2.8.6:60100c34` | On a `403` or `429`, a party SHOULD send `Accept-Signature` (RFC 9421 § 5.1) naming the components and parameters of § 2.8.2, and on a `503` it sends `Retry-After` instead. |
| `rule:2.8.7:bb44896e` | The private keys in its `keys.json` are public test keys: section 6.8 says they "MUST NOT be used in production", and a verifier SHOULD reject them. |
| `rule:2.8:909151f9` | A party MAY require a signature on the routes it serves. |
| `rule:2.8:cc948a48` | Signing is OPTIONAL in this specification: § 4.1.2 states what a signature shows, and `agreed` never depends on one. |
| `rule:2:ddbf85da` | A buyer MAY select and commit any subset of a proposal's `line_items[]`. |

| `rule:4.1.1:6e3d2c89` | A producer MUST NOT write a Number that this serialization does not reproduce as the numeric value written; a Number with at most 15 significant digits inside the range of a double always does, and a document containing one that does not is invalid for this section. |
| `rule:4.1.1:e93caddf` | A receiving party MUST recompute `record_digest` and compare it byte-for-byte before relying on a live `commitment` entry, over the line items as they stand plus the writes its `commit` carries (§ 5.6.1), or on a matching pair, from its agreed record (§ 4.1.2). |
| `rule:4.1.1:e95eca3f` | Strings, `Instant` values included, are hashed as that value; a party MUST NOT trim, normalize, or reformat a value it did not write. |

| `rule:4.1.3:03718402` | Each change to the offered terms of a frozen version MUST create version `n+1` and MUST append exactly one `negotiation_history[]` entry carrying that `version`, with `actor: seller` and `paths[]` naming every offered-terms field whose value differs from version `n`. |
| `rule:4.1.3:862b74db` | A seller MUST keep every version that a `negotiation_history[]` or `assent[]` entry names retrievable by `(proposal_id, version)` for as long as the proposal is retrievable. |
| `rule:4.1.3:dd69dac7` | A seller MAY edit a proposal in place only while its current version is not yet frozen (§ 4.1). |
| `rule:4.1.3:e06c57e1` | A line item that appears in a frozen version MUST NOT be removed from the proposal or moved to another package; it leaves the shelf by its `status` (§ 5.1), which is a lifecycle change. |
| `rule:4.1.3:fb70b3cb` | The following are not offered terms and MAY change without a new version: `status` (proposal and line item), `assent[]`, `negotiation_history[]`, `change_requests[]`, `availability`, `addressable_scale`, `hold`, `external_references[]`, `assets[].approval_status`, a `built_by: seller` entry of `assets[]`; `best_for[]`, `not_suitable_for[]`, and `seasonality_notes`, which are advisory (§ 4.2); `pricing[].min_rate`, which is a hint; `buyer_supplied.expected_match_rate`, which is the seller's stated expectation; every value the buyer writes (a `BuyerWrites` value, `commitment`, any other `supplied` field, and a `requestable` ask); and every `derived` value computed from a buyer's writes (`pricing[].agreed_rate`, `commitment.derived_units`). |
| `rule:4.1.4:24987106` | A `commit` that names an add-on MUST name every line item in its package's `line_items[]` and meet each of the package's `min_commitments[]`. |

| `rule:5.4.1:11d3ac1e` | Synthetic events MAY be sent to test endpoints, or through a test mode that the receiving service documents as excluding those events from production measurement and delivery diagnostics. |
| `rule:5.4.1:3a29e732` | An implementation MUST NOT report a check that was skipped or could not finish as passed. |
| `rule:5.4.1:52bc805d` | The inspector MUST NOT request a destination or redirect that sends a production notification; it MAY use a separate inspection URL or the receiving service's documented test mode under the conditions below. |
| `rule:5.4.1:725c4a7c` | If the service cannot provide such a session, the creative MUST be inspected outside the stitched stream. |
| `rule:5.4.1:7adf48a7` | An implementation performing inspection MUST NOT send any production notification. |
| `rule:5.4.1:9a1bf7bd` | A preview served through server-side ad insertion MUST use a session in which the insertion service sends no production notifications. |
| `rule:5.4.1:ef1a453e` | An implementation MAY retrieve creative documents, follow VAST wrappers and inspect referenced media as needed for its checks. |
| `rule:5.4.1:f25ad098` | A preview that executes creative or verification code MUST prevent that code from sending production notifications. |
| `rule:5.4.1:fbc60b60` | Inspection failures MUST be reported through diagnostics, without firing a production VAST `<Error>` or `verificationNotExecuted` URL. |
| `rule:5.4:28521712` | A `spec` MAY also hold `voice`, a `VocabularyTerm` that says whether a synthesized voice may read the ad. |
